Style sheet language

This article is about the markup styling language. For other uses, see [CSS (disambiguation)](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles").

**Cascading Style Sheets** (**CSS**) is a [style sheet language](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=ext.cite.parsoid.styles%7Cext.cite.styles%7Cext.pygments%7Cext.tmh.player.styles%7Cext.uls.interlanguage%7Cext.visualEditor.desktopArticleTarget.noscript%7Cext.wikimediamessages.styles%7Cjquery.makeCollapsible.styles%7Cjquery.tablesorter.styles%7Cmediawiki.skinning.content.parsoid%7Cskins.vector.icons%2Cstyles%7Cskins.vector.search.codex.styles%7Cwikibase.client.init&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ext.globalCssJs.user.styles") used for specifying the [presentation](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=ext.cite.parsoid.styles%7Cext.cite.styles%7Cext.pygments%7Cext.tmh.player.styles%7Cext.uls.interlanguage%7Cext.visualEditor.desktopArticleTarget.noscript%7Cext.wikimediamessages.styles%7Cjquery.makeCollapsible.styles%7Cjquery.tablesorter.styles%7Cmediawiki.skinning.content.parsoid%7Cskins.vector.icons%2Cstyles%7Cskins.vector.search.codex.styles%7Cwikibase.client.init&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ext.globalCssJs.user.styles") and styling of a document written in a [markup language](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=ext.cite.parsoid.styles%7Cext.cite.styles%7Cext.pygments%7Cext.tmh.player.styles%7Cext.uls.interlanguage%7Cext.visualEditor.desktopArticleTarget.noscript%7Cext.wikimediamessages.styles%7Cjquery.makeCollapsible.styles%7Cjquery.tablesorter.styles%7Cmediawiki.skinning.content.parsoid%7Cskins.vector.icons%2Cstyles%7Cskins.vector.search.codex.styles%7Cwikibase.client.init&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ext.globalCssJs.user.styles"), such as [HTML](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=ext.cite.parsoid.styles%7Cext.cite.styles%7Cext.pygments%7Cext.tmh.player.styles%7Cext.uls.interlanguage%7Cext.visualEditor.desktopArticleTarget.noscript%7Cext.wikimediamessages.styles%7Cjquery.makeCollapsible.styles%7Cjquery.tablesorter.styles%7Cmediawiki.skinning.content.parsoid%7Cskins.vector.icons%2Cstyles%7Cskins.vector.search.codex.styles%7Cwikibase.client.init&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles") or [XML](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=ext.cite.parsoid.styles%7Cext.cite.styles%7Cext.pygments%7Cext.tmh.player.styles%7Cext.uls.interlanguage%7Cext.visualEditor.desktopArticleTarget.noscript%7Cext.wikimediamessages.styles%7Cjquery.makeCollapsible.styles%7Cjquery.tablesorter.styles%7Cmediawiki.skinning.content.parsoid%7Cskins.vector.icons%2Cstyles%7Cskins.vector.search.codex.styles%7Cwikibase.client.init&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles") (including XML dialects such as [SVG](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=ext.cite.parsoid.styles%7Cext.cite.styles%7Cext.pygments%7Cext.tmh.player.styles%7Cext.uls.interlanguage%7Cext.visualEditor.desktopArticleTarget.noscript%7Cext.wikimediamessages.styles%7Cjquery.makeCollapsible.styles%7Cjquery.tablesorter.styles%7Cmediawiki.skinning.content.parsoid%7Cskins.vector.icons%2Cstyles%7Cskins.vector.search.codex.styles%7Cwikibase.client.init&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles"), [MathML](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=ext.cite.parsoid.styles%7Cext.cite.styles%7Cext.pygments%7Cext.tmh.player.styles%7Cext.uls.interlanguage%7Cext.visualEditor.desktopArticleTarget.noscript%7Cext.wikimediamessages.styles%7Cjquery.makeCollapsible.styles%7Cjquery.tablesorter.styles%7Cmediawiki.skinning.content.parsoid%7Cskins.vector.icons%2Cstyles%7Cskins.vector.search.codex.styles%7Cwikibase.client.init&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles"), or [XHTML](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=ext.cite.parsoid.styles%7Cext.cite.styles%7Cext.pygments%7Cext.tmh.player.styles%7Cext.uls.interlanguage%7Cext.visualEditor.desktopArticleTarget.noscript%7Cext.wikimediamessages.styles%7Cjquery.makeCollapsible.styles%7Cjquery.tablesorter.styles%7Cmediawiki.skinning.content.parsoid%7Cskins.vector.icons%2Cstyles%7Cskins.vector.search.codex.styles%7Cwikibase.client.init&only=styles&skin=vector-2022
"ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles")).[[2]](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=ext.cite.parsoid.styles%7Cext.cite.styles%7Cext.pygments%7Cext.tmh.player.styles%7Cext.uls.interlanguage%7Cext.visualEditor.desktopArticleTarget.noscript%7Cext.wikimediamessages.styles%7Cjquery.makeCollapsible.styles%7Cjquery.tablesorter.styles%7Cmediawiki.skinning.content.parsoid%7Cskins.vector.icons%2Cstyles%7Cskins.vector.search.codex.styles%7Cwikibase.client.init&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles")
CSS is a cornerstone technology of the [World Wide Web](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=ext.cite.parsoid.styles%7Cext.cite.styles%7Cext.pygments%7Cext.tmh.player.styles%7Cext.uls.interlanguage%7Cext.visualEditor.desktopArticleTarget.noscript%7Cext.wikimediamessages.styles%7Cjquery.makeCollapsible.styles%7Cjquery.tablesorter.styles%7Cmediawiki.skinning.content.parsoid%7Cskins.vector.icons%2Cstyles%7Cskins.vector.search.codex.styles%7Cwikibase.client.init&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles"), alongside HTML and [JavaScript](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=ext.cite.parsoid.styles%7Cext.cite.styles%7Cext.pygments%7Cext.tmh.player.styles%7Cext.uls.interlanguage%7Cext.visualEditor.desktopArticleTarget.noscript%7Cext.wikimediamessages.styles%7Cjquery.makeCollapsible.styles%7Cjquery.tablesorter.styles%7Cmediawiki.skinning.content.parsoid%7Cskins.vector.icons%2Cstyles%7Cskins.vector.search.codex.styles%7Cwikibase.client.init&only=styles&skin=vector-2022
"ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles").[[3]](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=ext.cite.parsoid.styles%7Cext.cite.styles%7Cext.pygments%7Cext.tmh.player.styles%7Cext.uls.interlanguage%7Cext.visualEditor.desktopArticleTarget.noscript%7Cext.wikimediamessages.styles%7Cjquery.makeCollapsible.styles%7Cjquery.tablesorter.styles%7Cmediawiki.skinning.content.parsoid%7Cskins.vector.icons%2Cstyles%7Cskins.vector.search.codex.styles%7Cwikibase.client.init&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles")

CSS is designed to enable the [separation of content and presentation](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=ext.cite.parsoid.styles%7Cext.cite.styles%7Cext.pygments%7Cext.tmh.player.styles%7Cext.uls.interlanguage%7Cext.visualEditor.desktopArticleTarget.noscript%7Cext.wikimediamessages.styles%7Cjquery.makeCollapsible.styles%7Cjquery.tablesorter.styles%7Cmediawiki.skinning.content.parsoid%7Cskins.vector.icons%2Cstyles%7Cskins.vector.search.codex.styles%7Cwikibase.client.init&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles"), including [layout](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=ext.cite.parsoid.styles%7Cext.cite.styles%7Cext.pygments%7Cext.tmh.player.styles%7Cext.uls.interlanguage%7Cext.visualEditor.desktopArticleTarget.noscript%7Cext.wikimediamessages.styles%7Cjquery.makeCollapsible.styles%7Cjquery.tablesorter.styles%7Cmediawiki.skinning.content.parsoid%7Cskins.vector.icons%2Cstyles%7Cskins.vector.search.codex.styles%7Cwikibase.client.init&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles"), [colors](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=ext.cite.parsoid.styles%7Cext.cite.styles%7Cext.pygments%7Cext.tmh.player.styles%7Cext.uls.interlanguage%7Cext.visualEditor.desktopArticleTarget.noscript%7Cext.wikimediamessages.styles%7Cjquery.makeCollapsible.styles%7Cjquery.tablesorter.styles%7Cmediawiki.skinning.content.parsoid%7Cskins.vector.icons%2Cstyles%7Cskins.vector.search.codex.styles%7Cwikibase.client.init&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles"), and [fonts](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=ext.cite.parsoid.styles%7Cext.cite.styles%7Cext.pygments%7Cext.tmh.player.styles%7Cext.uls.interlanguage%7Cext.visualEditor.desktopArticleTarget.noscript%7Cext.wikimediamessages.styles%7Cjquery.makeCollapsible.styles%7Cjquery.tablesorter.styles%7Cmediawiki.skinning.content.parsoid%7Cskins.vector.icons%2Cstyles%7Cskins.vector.search.codex.styles%7Cwikibase.client.init&only=styles&skin=vector-2022
"ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles").[[4]](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=ext.cite.parsoid.styles%7Cext.cite.styles%7Cext.pygments%7Cext.tmh.player.styles%7Cext.uls.interlanguage%7Cext.visualEditor.desktopArticleTarget.noscript%7Cext.wikimediamessages.styles%7Cjquery.makeCollapsible.styles%7Cjquery.tablesorter.styles%7Cmediawiki.skinning.content.parsoid%7Cskins.vector.icons%2Cstyles%7Cskins.vector.search.codex.styles%7Cwikibase.client.init&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles")
This separation can improve content [accessibility](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=ext.cite.parsoid.styles%7Cext.cite.styles%7Cext.pygments%7Cext.tmh.player.styles%7Cext.uls.interlanguage%7Cext.visualEditor.desktopArticleTarget.noscript%7Cext.wikimediamessages.styles%7Cjquery.makeCollapsible.styles%7Cjquery.tablesorter.styles%7Cmediawiki.skinning.content.parsoid%7Cskins.vector.icons%2Cstyles%7Cskins.vector.search.codex.styles%7Cwikibase.client.init&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles"), since the content can be written without concern for its presentation; provide more flexibility and control in the specification of presentation characteristics; enable multiple [web pages](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=ext.cite.parsoid.styles%7Cext.cite.styles%7Cext.pygments%7Cext.tmh.player.styles%7Cext.uls.interlanguage%7Cext.visualEditor.desktopArticleTarget.noscript%7Cext.wikimediamessages.styles%7Cjquery.makeCollapsible.styles%7Cjquery.tablesorter.styles%7Cmediawiki.skinning.content.parsoid%7Cskins.vector.icons%2Cstyles%7Cskins.vector.search.codex.styles%7Cwikibase.client.init&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles") to share formatting by specifying the relevant CSS in a separate .css file, which reduces complexity and repetition in the structural content; and enable the .css file to be [cached](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=ext.cite.parsoid.styles%7Cext.cite.styles%7Cext.pygments%7Cext.tmh.player.styles%7Cext.uls.interlanguage%7Cext.visualEditor.desktopArticleTarget.noscript%7Cext.wikimediamessages.styles%7Cjquery.makeCollapsible.styles%7Cjquery.tablesorter.styles%7Cmediawiki.skinning.content.parsoid%7Cskins.vector.icons%2Cstyles%7Cskins.vector.search.codex.styles%7Cwikibase.client.init&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles") to improve the page load speed between the pages that share the file and its formatting.

Separation of formatting and content also makes it feasible to present the same markup
page in different styles for different rendering methods, such as on-screen, in print,
by voice (via speech-based browser or [screen reader](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=ext.cite.parsoid.styles%7Cext.cite.styles%7Cext.pygments%7Cext.tmh.player.styles%7Cext.uls.interlanguage%7Cext.visualEditor.desktopArticleTarget.noscript%7Cext.wikimediamessages.styles%7Cjquery.makeCollapsible.styles%7Cjquery.tablesorter.styles%7Cmediawiki.skinning.content.parsoid%7Cskins.vector.icons%2Cstyles%7Cskins.vector.search.codex.styles%7Cwikibase.client.init&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles")), and on [Braille-based](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=ext.cite.parsoid.styles%7Cext.cite.styles%7Cext.pygments%7Cext.tmh.player.styles%7Cext.uls.interlanguage%7Cext.visualEditor.desktopArticleTarget.noscript%7Cext.wikimediamessages.styles%7Cjquery.makeCollapsible.styles%7Cjquery.tablesorter.styles%7Cmediawiki.skinning.content.parsoid%7Cskins.vector.icons%2Cstyles%7Cskins.vector.search.codex.styles%7Cwikibase.client.init&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles") tactile devices. CSS also has rules for alternative formatting if the content is accessed on a [mobile device](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=ext.cite.parsoid.styles%7Cext.cite.styles%7Cext.pygments%7Cext.tmh.player.styles%7Cext.uls.interlanguage%7Cext.visualEditor.desktopArticleTarget.noscript%7Cext.wikimediamessages.styles%7Cjquery.makeCollapsible.styles%7Cjquery.tablesorter.styles%7Cmediawiki.skinning.content.parsoid%7Cskins.vector.icons%2Cstyles%7Cskins.vector.search.codex.styles%7Cwikibase.client.init&only=styles&skin=vector-2022
"ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles").[[5]](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=ext.cite.parsoid.styles%7Cext.cite.styles%7Cext.pygments%7Cext.tmh.player.styles%7Cext.uls.interlanguage%7Cext.visualEditor.desktopArticleTarget.noscript%7Cext.wikimediamessages.styles%7Cjquery.makeCollapsible.styles%7Cjquery.tablesorter.styles%7Cmediawiki.skinning.content.parsoid%7Cskins.vector.icons%2Cstyles%7Cskins.vector.search.codex.styles%7Cwikibase.client.init&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles")

The name *cascading* comes from the specified priority scheme to determine which
declaration applies if more than one declaration of a property match a particular
element. This cascading priority scheme is predictable.

The CSS specifications are maintained by the [World Wide Web Consortium](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles") (W3C). Internet media type ([MIME type](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles")) `text/css`
is registered for use with CSS by RFC 2318 (March 1998). The W3C operates a free
[CSS validation service](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles") for CSS
documents.[[6]](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles")

In addition to HTML, other markup languages support the use of CSS including [XHTML](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles"), [plain XML](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles"), [SVG](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles"), and [XUL](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles"). CSS is also used in the [GTK](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles") [widget toolkit](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles").


## Syntax
[[edit](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022
"ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles")]
CSS has a simple [syntax](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles") and uses a number of English keywords to specify the names of various style properties.


### Style sheet
[[edit](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022
"ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles")]

Main article: [Style sheet (web development)](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles")
A style sheet consists of a list of *rules*. Each rule or rule-set consists of one
or more *[selectors](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022
"ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles")*, and a *[declaration block](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022
"ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles")*.


### Selector
[[edit](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022
"ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles")]

"CSS class" redirects here. For element classes that are part of the HTML content
model upon which the CSS class selector syntax depends, see [class attribute (HTML)](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles").
In CSS, *selectors* declare which part of the markup a style applies to by matching
tags and attributes in the markup itself.


#### Selector types
[[edit](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022
"ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles")]
Selectors may apply to the following:

- all [elements](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles") of a specific type, e.g. the second-level headers [h2](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles")
- elements specified by [attribute](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles"), in particular:- *id*: an identifier unique within the document, denoted in the selector language by a [hash](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles") prefix e.g. `#id`
- *class*: an identifier that can annotate multiple elements in a document, denoted by a [dot](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles") prefix e.g. `.classname` (the phrase "CSS class", although sometimes used, is a misnomer, as element classes—specified with the [HTML class attribute](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles")—is a markup feature that is distinct from browsers' CSS subsystems, and the related W3C/WHATWG standards work on document styles; see [RDF](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles") and [microformats](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles") for the origins of the "class" system of the Web content model)
- elements depending on how they are placed relative to others in the [document tree](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles").

Classes and IDs are case-sensitive, start with letters, and can include alphanumeric
characters, hyphens, and underscores. A class may apply to any number of instances
of any element. An ID may only be applied to a single element.


#### Pseudo-classes
[[edit](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022
"ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles")]

"Pseudo-element" redirects here. For pseudoelement symbols in chemistry, see [Skeletal formula
§ Pseudoelement symbols](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles").
*Pseudo-classes* are used in CSS selectors to permit formatting based on information
that is not contained in the document tree.

One example of a widely used pseudo-class is `:hover`, which identifies content only when
the user "points to" the visible element, usually by holding the mouse cursor over
it. It is appended to a selector as in `a:hover` or `#elementid:hover`.

A pseudo-class classifies document elements, such as `:link` or `:visited`, whereas a *pseudo-element*
makes a selection that may consist of partial elements, such as `::first-line` or `::first-letter`.[[7]](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles")
Note the distinction between the double-colon notation used for pseudo-elements and
the single-colon notation used for pseudo-classes.


#### Combinators
[[edit](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022
"ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles")]
Multiple simple selectors may be joined using combinators to specify elements by
location, element type, id, class, or any combination thereof.[[8]](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles")
The order of the selectors is important. For example, `div .myClass {color: red;}` applies to all elements
of class myClass that are inside div elements, whereas `.myClass div {color: red;}` applies to all div elements
that are inside elements of class myClass. This is not to be confused with concatenated
identifiers such as `div.myClass {color: red;}` which applies to div elements of class myClass.


#### Summary of selector syntax
[[edit](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022
"ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles")]
The following table provides a summary of selector syntax indicating usage and the
version of CSS that introduced it.[[9]](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles")

| CSS selectors                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
|------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|-------------------------------|
| -                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | -                                                                                                                                  | -                             |
| `E`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | an element of type E                                                                                                               | 1                             |
| `E:link`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | an E element that is the source anchor of a hyperlink whose target is either not yet visited (:link) or already visited (:visited) | 1                             |
| `E:visited`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `E:active`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | an E element during certain user actions                                                                                           | 1                             |
| `E:hover`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | 2                                                                                                                                  |
| `E:focus`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `E::first-line`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | the first formatted line of an E element                                                                                           | 1                             |
| `E::first-letter`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | the first formatted letter of an E element                                                                                         | 1                             |
| `.c`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | all elements with class="c"                                                                                                        | 1                             |
| `#myid`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | the element with id="myid"                                                                                                         | 1                             |
| `E.warning`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | an E element whose class is "warning" (the document language specifies how class is determined)                                    | 1                             |
| `E#myid`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | an E element with ID equal to "myid"                                                                                               | 1                             |
| `.c#myid`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | the element with class="c" and ID equal to "myid"                                                                                  | 1                             |
| `E F`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | an F element descendant of an E element                                                                                            | 1                             |
| `*`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | any element                                                                                                                        | 2                             |
| `E[foo]`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | an E element with a "foo" attribute                                                                                                | 2                             |
| `E[foo="bar"]`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | an E element whose "foo" attribute value is exactly equal to "bar"                                                                 | 2                             |
| `E[foo~="bar"]`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | an E element whose "foo" attribute value is a list of whitespace-separated values, one of which is exactly equal to "bar"          | 2                             |
| `[foo                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | =\"en\"]"},"2":{"wt":"css"}},"i":0}}]}">E[foo                               | ="en"]`                       | an E element whose "foo" attribute has a hyphen-separated list of values beginning (from the left) with "en" | 2 |
| `E:first-child`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | an E element, first child of its parent                                                                                            | 2                             |
| `E:lang(fr)`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | an element of type E in language "fr" (the document language specifies how language is determined)                                 | 2                             |
| `E::before`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | generated content before an E element's content                                                                                    | 2                             |
| `E::after`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | generated content after an E element's content                                                                                     | 2                             |
| ` F"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"code","href":"./Template:Code"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"E > F"},"2":{"wt":"css"}},"i":0}}]}">E > F` | an F element child of an E element                                                                                                 | 2                             |
| `E + F`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | an F element immediately preceded by an E element                                                                                  | 2                             |
| `E[foo^="bar"]`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | an E element whose "foo" attribute value begins exactly with the string "bar"                                                      | 3                             |
| `E[foo$="bar"]`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | an E element whose "foo" attribute value ends exactly with the string "bar"                                                        | 3                             |
| `E[foo*="bar"]`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | an E element whose "foo" attribute value contains the substring "bar"                                                              | 3                             |
| `E:root`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | an E element, root of the document                                                                                                 | 3                             |
| `E:nth-child(n)`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | an E element, the n-th child of its parent                                                                                         | 3                             |
| `E:nth-last-child(n)`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | an E element, the n-th child of its parent, counting from the last one                                                             | 3                             |
| `E:nth-of-type(n)`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | an E element, the n-th sibling of its type                                                                                         | 3                             |
| `E:nth-last-of-type(n)`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | an E element, the n-th sibling of its type, counting from the last one                                                             | 3                             |
| `E:last-child`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | an E element, last child of its parent                                                                                             | 3                             |
| `E:first-of-type`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | an E element, first sibling of its type                                                                                            | 3                             |
| `E:last-of-type`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | an E element, last sibling of its type                                                                                             | 3                             |
| `E:only-child`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | an E element, only child of its parent                                                                                             | 3                             |
| `E:only-of-type`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | an E element, only sibling of its type                                                                                             | 3                             |
| `E:empty`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | an E element that has no children (including text nodes)                                                                           | 3                             |
| `E:target`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | an E element being the target of the referring URI                                                                                 | 3                             |
| `E:enabled`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | a user interface element E that is enabled                                                                                         | 3                             |
